# List 17

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>nuance</b></td><td>/ˈnuːɑːns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>细微的差异</td><td>subtle nuances of meaning<br>意义的微妙差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Translators must capture subtle nuances of meaning that dictionaries rarely explain well.  译者必须捕捉词典很少能解释清楚的微妙意义差异。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patent difference</td><td>/ˈpætənt ˈdɪfərəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>明显的差别</td><td>a patent difference in tone<br>语气上的明显差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a patent difference between the two translations.  两个译本之间有明显的差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lack of subtlety</td><td>/ˈlæk əv ˈsʌtəlti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏细腻，不含蓄</td><td>a lack of subtlety in color<br>用色缺乏细腻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics noted a lack of subtlety in his later work.  评论者指出他后期作品缺乏细腻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sharp distinction</td><td>/ˈʃɑrp dɪˈstɪŋkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鲜明的区别</td><td>draw a sharp distinction<br>作出鲜明区分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author draws a sharp distinction between guilt and shame.  作者对罪感与羞耻作出鲜明区分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patency</td><td>/ˈpeɪtənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>明显，显而易见</td><td>the patency of the error<br>错误之明显</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patency of the contradiction embarrassed the whole committee.  矛盾如此明显，让整个委员会尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>nudge</b></td><td>/nʌdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>说服某人做某事</td><td>nudge him into agreeing<br>说服他同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her patient questions nudged him into agreeing to see a doctor.  她耐心的询问说服他同意去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exhort</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>敦促，力劝</td><td>exhort them to persevere<br>力劝他们坚持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker exhorted the students to persevere despite repeated failures.  演讲者勉励学生们即使屡遭失败也要坚持下去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goad</td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 press</td><td>/ˈpres/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>按，压；催促</td><td>press the button<br>按下按钮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He pressed the button twice before the lift moved.  他按了两次按钮电梯才动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prod</td><td>/prɑːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促使…行动</td><td>prod her into action<br>促使她行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her friends had to prod her into applying for the scholarship.  朋友们不得不促使她去申请那份奖学金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prompt</td><td>/ˈprɑːmpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促使，引起；提示</td><td>prompt an inquiry<br>引发一场调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaked memo prompted an immediate inquiry by regulators.  泄露的备忘录促使监管机构立即展开调查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>nugatory</b></del></td><td>/ˈnuːɡətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的</td><td>a nugatory objection<br>无关紧要的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court dismissed his nugatory objection and proceeded with the hearing.  法庭驳回了他无关紧要的反对，继续进行听证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incidental</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪˈdentəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶然的；附带的</td><td>incidental expenses<br>杂项开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The damage was incidental to the main purpose of the test.  这种损坏只是测试主要目的之外的附带结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconsequential</td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːnsɪˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的，微不足道的</td><td>an inconsequential remark<br>无关紧要的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The difference between the two prices is quite inconsequential.  两个价格之间的差别微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconsiderable</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的</td><td>an inconsiderable sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk is small but by no means inconsiderable.  风险虽小，却绝非微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insignificant</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的，微不足道的</td><td>a seemingly insignificant change<br>看似微小的改动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An insignificant error early on ruined the entire calculation.  早期一个微不足道的错误毁了整个计算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trifling</td><td>/ˈtraɪflɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，不重要的</td><td>a trifling sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dismissed the missing coins as a trifling sum not worth reporting.  他把丢失的硬币当作微不足道的小钱，不值得上报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consequential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；随之而来的</td><td>a consequential ruling<br>一项重要的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That quiet vote proved the most consequential act of the session.  那次不起眼的投票成了本届会议最重要的举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eventful</td><td>/ɪˈventfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多事的，重要的</td><td>an eventful year<br>多事的一年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It has been an eventful year for the whole family.  对全家来说这是多事的一年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 important</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpɔːrtənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的</td><td>an important decision<br>一个重要的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sleep is as important as diet for a young athlete.  对年轻运动员来说，睡眠和饮食一样重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 major</td><td>/ˈmeɪdʒər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，主要的</td><td>a major concern<br>主要的关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water shortage remains a major concern for the region.  缺水仍是该地区的主要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meaningful</td><td>/ˈmiːnɪŋfəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有意义的，意味深长的</td><td>meaningful work<br>有意义的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She left banking to find more meaningful work.  她离开银行业去寻找更有意义的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 momentous</td><td>/moʊˈmentəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重大的，重要的</td><td>a momentous decision<br>一个重大的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board reached a momentous decision that changed the company's direction entirely.  董事会做出了一个重大决定，彻底改变了公司的方向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 significant</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；显著的</td><td>a significant difference<br>显著的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a significant drop in the pollution levels.  研究者发现污染水平显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 substantial</td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unfrivolous</del></td><td>/ʌnˈfrɪvələs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的，不轻浮的</td><td>an unfrivolous objection<br>严肃的反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an unfrivolous objection that delayed the vote.  她提出一个严肃的反对意见，推迟了表决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weighty</td><td>/ˈweɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的；沉重的</td><td>weighty matters<br>重大的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The elders debated weighty questions of land and water.  长者们讨论着土地与水源的重大问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>nurture</b></td><td>/ˈnɜːrtʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>培育，培养</td><td>nurture young talent<br>培养年轻人才</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers nurture curiosity rather than simply rewarding correct answers.  好老师培育好奇心，而不只是奖励正确答案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 edify</td><td>/ˈedəˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>教化，启发</td><td>edify the young<br>教化年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lectures were meant to edify rather than entertain.  这些讲座意在教化而非娱乐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inspire</td><td>/ɪnˈspaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓舞，激励；引发</td><td>inspire confidence in others<br>让他人产生信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech inspired the students to work much harder.  她的演讲激励学生更加努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stunt</td><td>/stʌnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍（成长）</td><td>stunt the growth<br>阻碍生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor nutrition can stunt the growth of children in their early years.  营养不良会阻碍儿童早年的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obtrusive</b></td><td>/əbˈtruːsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扎眼的，难看显眼的</td><td>an obtrusive advertisement<br>扎眼的广告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obtrusive advertisement covered half the historic facade of the old theater.  一幅扎眼的广告遮住了老剧院半面历史立面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intruding</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtruːdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>侵入的，打扰的</td><td>an intruding noise<br>扰人的噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An intruding noise from the street spoiled the recording.  街上扰人的噪音毁了这次录音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prominent</td><td>/ˈprɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的；显眼的</td><td>a prominent local politician<br>当地知名的政治人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name appears in a prominent place on the donor list.  她的名字出现在捐赠名单的显眼位置。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 highlighted</td><td>/ˈhaɪˌlaɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>被突出的，被强调的</td><td>the highlighted passage<br>被标出的段落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please read the highlighted passage on the second page.  请读第二页被标出的段落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 striking</td><td>/ˈstraɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人注目的，显著的</td><td>a striking resemblance<br>惊人的相似</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a striking contrast between the two sisters' styles.  两姐妹的风格形成鲜明的对比。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unobtrusive</td><td>/ˌʌnəbˈtruːsɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不显眼的，不张扬的</td><td>unobtrusive service<br>不张扬的服务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel's unobtrusive service won it loyal guests.  这家酒店不张扬的服务赢得了忠实客人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obdurate</b></td><td>/ˈɑːbdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；冷酷无情的</td><td>remain obdurate<br>仍然固执不改</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord was obdurate and refused to lower the rent.  房东顽固不化，拒绝降低房租。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 hard - nosed</del></td><td>/ˈhɑrd ˈnoʊzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强硬的，不讲情面的</td><td>a hard-nosed negotiator<br>强硬的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hard-nosed negotiator refused every early offer.  他们强硬的谈判代表拒绝了所有早期报价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intransigent</td><td>/ɪnˈtrænzɪdʒənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，固执的</td><td>an intransigent attitude<br>不妥协的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides remained intransigent, so the negotiations collapsed.  双方都毫不妥协，谈判因此破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pertinacious</del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrtnˈeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，固执地坚持的</td><td>pertinacious in her demands<br>固执地坚持要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pertinacious reporter kept calling until she got an answer.  这位执着的记者不停打电话，直到得到答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbending</td><td>/ʌnˈbendɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不亲切的；不妥协的</td><td>an unbending attitude<br>不肯通融的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old teacher was unbending about rules and manners.  这位老教师在规矩和礼节上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncompromising</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmprəmaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，坚定的</td><td>an uncompromising stance<br>毫不妥协的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took an uncompromising stand against corruption in the department.  她对部门内的腐败采取了毫不妥协的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrelenting</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不松懈的，不屈不挠的</td><td>unrelenting pressure<br>持续不断的压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unrelenting effort finally forced the company to apologize.  她不懈的努力最终迫使公司道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unyielding</td><td>/ʌnˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不屈的，不让步的</td><td>unyielding opposition<br>毫不让步的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union remained unyielding on the question of wages.  工会在工资问题上依然毫不让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 affectless</del></td><td>/ˈæfektləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无情感的，冷漠的</td><td>an affectless voice<br>毫无感情的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness described the crime in a flat, affectless tone.  证人用平淡无情感的语调描述这桩罪行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 callous</td><td>/ˈkæləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无同情心的，冷漠的</td><td>a callous remark<br>冷漠无情的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His callous joke about the accident shocked everyone in the room.  他拿这场事故开的冷酷玩笑震惊了房间里的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 compassionless</del></td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无同情心的，冷酷的</td><td>a compassionless verdict<br>冷酷的判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The compassionless verdict drew protests outside the courthouse.  这冷酷的判决在法院外引发抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hard-hearted</td><td>/ˈhɑrd ˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷酷无情的</td><td>a hard-hearted landlord<br>冷酷的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a hard-hearted landlord would evict them in winter.  只有冷酷的房东才会在冬天赶他们走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heartless</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无情的，狠心的</td><td>a heartless decision<br>无情的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was heartless to fire her the week before Christmas.  在圣诞节前一周解雇她真是无情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 indurate</del></td><td>/ˈɪndjʊreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变硬；使冷酷无情</td><td>indurate the heart<br>使心变得冷硬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of betrayal had indurated his heart against every plea for sympathy.  多年的背叛使他的心变得冷硬，对任何求情都无动于衷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhuman</td><td>/ˌɪnˈhjuːmən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>残忍的，无人性的</td><td>inhuman treatment<br>残忍的对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report described inhuman treatment inside the camp.  报告描述了营地内残忍的对待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensate</td><td>/ɪnˈsenseɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无感觉的；无理性的</td><td>insensate to pain<br>对疼痛毫无感觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lay insensate on the floor for several minutes.  他失去知觉地在地板上躺了好几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ironhearted</del></td><td>/ˈaɪərnˌhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>铁石心肠的</td><td>an ironhearted commander<br>铁石心肠的指挥官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even the ironhearted commander hesitated before that order.  连铁石心肠的指挥官在那道命令前也犹豫了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merciless</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无仁慈的，无情的</td><td>a merciless critic<br>毫不留情的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merciless summer sun beat down on the empty streets.  无情的夏日阳光炙烤着空荡的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remorseless</td><td>/rɪˈmɔːrsləs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无悔意的，冷酷无情的</td><td>remorseless cruelty<br>冷酷无情的残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The killer remained remorseless throughout the long public trial.  凶手在漫长的公开审判中始终毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruthless</td><td>/ˈruːθləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有怜悯心的，残忍的</td><td>a ruthless dictator<br>残忍无情的独裁者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general was ruthless with anyone who questioned his orders.  将军对任何质疑其命令的人都残忍无情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsparing</td><td>/ʌnˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不留情的；毫不吝惜的</td><td>unsparing criticism<br>毫不留情的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was unsparing about the actor's weak performance.  这篇评论对该演员糟糕的表演毫不留情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquiescent</td><td>/ˌækwiˈesnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，默许的</td><td>an acquiescent audience<br>顺从的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too acquiescent to protest, the staff accepted the new rules.  员工太顺从，没有抗议就接受了新规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreeable</td><td>/əˈɡriːəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉悦的；相一致的</td><td>an agreeable companion<br>一个讨人喜欢的同伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found him an agreeable companion on the long train journey.  她觉得他是长途火车旅程中一位讨人喜欢的同伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 complying</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，遵从的</td><td>complying with the rules<br>遵从规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firms complying with the standard receive a tax rebate.  遵从该标准的企业可获得退税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relenting</td><td>/rɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>态度软化的，让步的</td><td>show a relenting attitude<br>表现出软化的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of pleading, his relenting expression gave us hope.  在几小时的哀求之后，他态度软化的表情给了我们希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compassionate</td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有同情心的，慈悲的</td><td>compassionate care<br>充满关怀的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A compassionate nurse sat with the frightened child all night.  一位有同情心的护士整夜陪着那个受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humane</td><td>/hjuˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>人道的，仁慈的</td><td>humane treatment of prisoners<br>人道地对待囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Activists demanded more humane conditions inside the detention center.  活动人士要求改善拘留中心内的人道条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kindhearted</td><td>/ˌkaɪndˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好心的，仁慈的</td><td>a kindhearted neighbor<br>好心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A kindhearted stranger paid for the child's bus ticket.  一位好心的陌生人替那孩子付了车票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kindly</td><td>/ˈkaɪndli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>和善的；亲切地</td><td>a kindly old man<br>一位和善的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A kindly neighbor carried her groceries up the steep stairs.  一位和善的邻居帮她把杂货搬上陡楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merciful</td><td>/ˈmɜːrsɪfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>仁慈的，宽厚的</td><td>a merciful judge<br>宽仁的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court was merciful and gave him only a small fine.  法庭很宽仁，只罚了他少许款项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 softhearted</del></td><td>/ˈsɔftˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心软的，慈悲的</td><td>a softhearted judge<br>心软的法官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The softhearted judge suspended most of the sentence.  心软的法官缓期执行了大部分刑罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sympathetic</td><td>/ˌsɪmpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同情的，有同情心的</td><td>a sympathetic listener<br>富有同情心的倾听者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse gave her a sympathetic look and held her hand.  护士同情地看着她，握住了她的手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 warmhearted</td><td>/ˈwɔrm ˈhɑrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热心的，热情友善的</td><td>a warmhearted neighbor<br>一位热心的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their warmhearted host insisted that everyone stay for dinner.  他们热心的主人坚持要所有人留下吃饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obedient</b></td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imperial</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪriəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，令人惊叹的</td><td>imperial grandeur<br>帝王般的宏伟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hall was decorated on an imperial scale that dazzled every visitor.  大厅以帝王般宏大的规模装饰，令每位来客惊叹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contrary</td><td>/kənˈtreri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，故意作对的</td><td>a contrary child<br>执拗的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contrary child refused whatever his mother suggested.  这执拗的孩子对母亲的建议一概拒绝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disobedient</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，不听话的</td><td>a disobedient child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disobedient dog ran off the moment the gate opened.  门一开，那只不听话的狗就跑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 froward</td><td>/ˈfroʊərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不服从的</td><td>a froward apprentice<br>倔强的学徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The froward apprentice ignored every warning from his master.  这倔强的学徒无视师父的每一次警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 incompliant</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顺从的，不依从的</td><td>an incompliant witness<br>不肯配合的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incompliant staff refused to follow the new dress rule.  不顺从的员工拒绝遵守新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noncompliant</td><td>/ˌnɑnkəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不遵守的，不服从的</td><td>noncompliant equipment<br>不合规的设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors shut down the noncompliant plant within a single day.  检查人员一天之内就关闭了这家不合规的工厂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebel</td><td>/rɪˈbel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>反抗，造反；反叛者</td><td>rebel against authority<br>反抗权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students rebelled against the strict new curfew last spring.  学生们去年春天反抗严苛的新宵禁令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 restive</td><td>/ˈrestɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的；不驯服的</td><td>a restive crowd<br>焦躁不安的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience became restive when the concert started forty minutes late.  音乐会迟了四十分钟才开始，观众变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ungovernable</td><td>/ʌnˈɡʌvərnəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以控制的，无法管束的</td><td>an ungovernable temper<br>无法控制的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd became ungovernable once the gates were opened.  大门一开，人群就变得无法控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 untoward</td><td>/ʌnˈtɔːrd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的；难以驯服的，难以驾驭的</td><td>an untoward incident<br>意外的不幸事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing untoward happened during the long flight across the ocean.  在这趟跨洋长途飞行中没有发生任何不幸的意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wayward</td><td>/ˈweɪwərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，不服管束的</td><td>a wayward son<br>不服管束的儿子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their wayward son ignored every rule his patient parents set.  他们不服管束的儿子无视父母耐心定下的每条规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obeisance</b></td><td>/oʊˈbiːsns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>敬礼，尊重</td><td>make obeisance to the king<br>向国王行礼致敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Court officials made obeisance to the king before presenting their petitions.  朝臣们在呈上请愿书之前向国王行礼致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impudent</td><td>/ˈɪmpjədənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>放肆大胆的，无礼的</td><td>an impudent remark<br>一句无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The impudent student argued with the professor in class.  这个无礼的学生在课上与教授争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imperious</td><td>/ɪmˈpɪriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，专横的</td><td>an imperious tone<br>专横的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her imperious manner made junior colleagues afraid to disagree.  她专横的态度让年轻同事不敢有异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obfuscate</b></td><td>/ˈɑːbfʌskeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使模糊</td><td>obfuscate the facts<br>把事实弄模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed determined to obfuscate the facts rather than explain them.  证人似乎一心要把事实弄模糊，而不是解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 becloud</del></td><td>/bɪˈklaʊd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使混乱</td><td>becloud the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Emotional appeals often becloud the real issue under serious discussion.  情感诉求常常把正在认真讨论的真问题弄模糊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 befog</del></td><td>/bɪˈfɔːɡ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使困惑</td><td>befog the argument<br>使论证变模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Technical jargon befogs the argument instead of making it clearer.  技术术语让论证更模糊，而非更清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blur</td><td>/blɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）变得朦胧，（使）变得不清楚；使不易理解</td><td>blur the distinction<br>模糊了界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New technology tends to blur the distinction between work and home.  新技术往往模糊了工作与家庭的界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloud</td><td>/ˈklaʊd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使蒙上阴影</td><td>cloud the issue<br>把问题搅浑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Emotional appeals only clouded the real issue.  情绪化的诉求只把真正的问题搅浑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fog</td><td>/ˈfɑːɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使困惑</td><td>fog the mirror<br>使镜子起雾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much detail fogged the memory of what mattered.  过多细节模糊了对要点的记忆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muddy</td><td>/ˈmʌdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>浑浊的，不清晰的；使难以理解</td><td>muddy the argument<br>把论点搅浑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vague statistics only muddy the argument instead of settling it.  含糊的统计数据只会把论点搅浑，而不能解决争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demystify</td><td>/diˈmɪstəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浅显易懂，阐明</td><td>demystify the process<br>阐明这一过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book demystifies statistics for readers who fear numbers.  这本书为怕数字的读者把统计学讲得浅显易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elucidate</td><td>/iˈluːsɪdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>elucidate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The footnotes elucidate the problem far better than the chapter itself does.  脚注比正文本身更好地阐明了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explain clearly</td><td>/ɪkˈspleɪn ˈklɪrli/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>清楚解释</td><td>explain clearly to beginners<br>向初学者清楚解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good teacher can explain clearly without simplifying too much.  好老师能清楚解释而不过度简化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lucid</td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obligatory</b></td><td>/əˈblɪɡətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制性的</td><td>obligatory military service<br>强制的兵役</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that country obligatory military service lasts for eighteen months.  在那个国家，强制的兵役要服十八个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compulsory</td><td>/kəmˈpʌlsəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制的，必修的</td><td>compulsory education<br>义务教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing a helmet is compulsory on every construction site.  在每个建筑工地戴安全帽都是强制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperative</td><td>/ɪmˈperətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>命令的，强制性的；迫切的</td><td>an imperative need<br>迫切的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is imperative that we repair the dam before the rains arrive.  在雨季来临前修好大坝是迫切必需的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involuntary</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非自愿的；无意识的</td><td>an involuntary reaction<br>不自觉的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blinking is an involuntary response to sudden light.  眨眼是对突然强光的不自觉反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peremptory</td><td>/pəˈremptəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的；专横傲慢的</td><td>a peremptory order<br>不容置疑的命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His peremptory tone left no room for questions or discussion.  他傲慢的语气不给任何提问或讨论的余地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 required</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>必需的，规定的</td><td>required reading<br>必读材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A signed form is required before the trip can begin.  出行前必须提交一份签好的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discretionary</td><td>/dɪˈskreʃəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自主决定的</td><td>discretionary spending<br>自主决定的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Managers have discretionary power over small purchases.  经理对小额采购有自主决定权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 selective</td><td>/səˈlektɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有选择性的，严格筛选的</td><td>a highly selective college<br>招生严格筛选的学院</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This college is highly selective and turns down most applicants.  这所学院筛选严格，拒绝了大多数申请者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elective</td><td>/ɪˈlektɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可自由选择的；选举的</td><td>an elective course<br>选修课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the surgery performed here is elective, not urgent.  这里做的多数手术是可选择的，而非紧急的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 optional</td><td>/ˈɑːpʃənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可选择的，非强制的</td><td>an optional extra course<br>一门选修的额外课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely optional.  参加晚间研讨会完全是自愿的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 voluntary</td><td>/ˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自愿的，志愿的</td><td>voluntary participation<br>自愿参与</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely voluntary.  晚间研讨会完全自愿参加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obliging</b></td><td>/əˈblaɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐于助人的</td><td>an obliging neighbor<br>乐于帮忙的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obliging neighbor watered our plants every day while we were away.  一位乐于帮忙的邻居在我们外出期间每天给植物浇水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accommodative</td><td>/əˈkɑːməˌdeɪtɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐于配合的，通融的</td><td>an accommodative attitude<br>通融的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord took an accommodative attitude toward late rent.  房东对迟交房租采取通融态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friendly</td><td>/ˈfrendli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友好的，亲切的</td><td>a friendly welcome<br>友好的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shopkeeper was friendly and remembered every regular customer.  店主很友好，记得每位常客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indulgent</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌldʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（对己）放纵的，（对他人）纵容的</td><td>an indulgent parent<br>溺爱孩子的家长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her indulgent grandparents let her eat whatever she wanted.  溺爱她的祖父母让她想吃什么就吃什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>oblique</b></td><td>/əˈbliːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>斜的</td><td>an oblique angle<br>斜角</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The roof meets the wall at an oblique angle that complicates repairs.  屋顶与墙以一个斜角相接，使修理变得复杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 askew</td><td>/əˈskjuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>不成直线的（地），歪的（地）</td><td>hang askew on the wall<br>歪斜地挂在墙上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting hung askew on the wall above the fireplace.  那幅画歪斜地挂在壁炉上方的墙上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cant</td><td>/ˈkænt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>行话；伪善之言</td><td>political cant<br>政治套话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was pure cant dressed up as principle.  他的演讲不过是伪装成原则的套话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inclined</td><td>/ˌɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾向于的，愿意的</td><td>inclined to believe<br>倾向于相信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am inclined to believe his version of the story.  我倾向于相信他讲的那个版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leaning</td><td>/ˈliːnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>倾斜的；倾向</td><td>a leaning tower<br>倾斜的塔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaning wall was propped up with heavy timbers.  倾斜的墙用粗木支撑起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 listing</td><td>/ˈlɪstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>（船）倾斜的；清单</td><td>a listing ship<br>倾斜的船</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The listing vessel was towed slowly back into the harbor.  那艘倾斜的船被慢慢拖回港口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slanted</td><td>/ˈslæntɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾斜的；有偏向的</td><td>slanted news coverage<br>有偏向的新闻报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report was clearly slanted in favor of the company.  这份报告明显偏向该公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sloping</td><td>/ˈsloʊpɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾斜的，有坡度的</td><td>a sloping roof<br>斜屋顶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain ran quickly off the steeply sloping roof.  雨水迅速从陡斜的屋顶流下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tilted</td><td>/ˈtɪltɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾斜的，歪的</td><td>a tilted head<br>歪着的头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She looked at me with her head tilted to one side.  她歪着头看我。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 direct</td><td>/dəˈrekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直接的，径直的</td><td>a direct flight<br>直飞航班</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ask for a direct answer instead of a long explanation.  要个直接的答复，而不是长篇解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 even</td><td>/ˈiːvən/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平坦的，均匀的</td><td>an even surface<br>平坦的表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sand the board until the surface feels perfectly even.  把板子打磨到表面完全平整。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 level</td><td>/ˈlevəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>水平的，平的</td><td>on level ground<br>在平地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pitch the tent on level ground away from the stream.  把帐篷搭在远离溪水的平地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straight</td><td>/ˈstreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直的，笔直的</td><td>a straight road<br>笔直的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drive straight ahead until you reach the second traffic light.  一直往前开，直到第二个红绿灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obliterate</b></td><td>/əˈblɪtəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抹去；彻底消灭</td><td>obliterate all traces of it<br>抹去它的一切痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The March blizzard obliterated all traces of the early spring flowers.  三月的暴风雪抹去了早春花朵的一切痕迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 efface</td><td>/ɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦掉，抹去；使不显眼</td><td>efface all traces<br>抹去所有痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time had effaced the inscription on the old gravestone.  时间已把这块老墓碑上的铭文磨去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eradicate</td><td>/ɪˈrædəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，消灭</td><td>eradicate rural poverty<br>根除农村贫困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vaccination campaigns helped eradicate smallpox across the world.  接种运动帮助在全世界根除了天花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expunge</td><td>/ɪkˈspʌndʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>删除，消除</td><td>expunge a criminal record<br>消除犯罪记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court agreed to expunge the conviction from her official file.  法庭同意将该定罪从她的正式档案中删除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 expurgate</del></td><td>/ˈekspərɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>净化（书等），删去（不当处）</td><td>expurgate a text<br>删改文本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editors expurgated several passages before the book reached young readers.  编辑在这本书交到小读者手中之前删去了几个段落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exterminate</td><td>/ɪkˈstɜːrmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，消灭</td><td>exterminate the entire colony<br>消灭整个群落</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team worked for months to exterminate the invasive beetles.  团队花了几个月消灭那些入侵的甲虫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extirpate</td><td>/ˈekstərˌpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>根除，灭绝</td><td>extirpate an invasive species<br>根除入侵物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers worked for years to extirpate the weed from their fields.  农民花了多年才把这种杂草从田里根除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wipe out</td><td>/ˈwaɪp ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>彻底消灭，抹去</td><td>wipe out the debt<br>抹去债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One bad season wiped out the family's savings.  一个糟糕的季节抹光了这家人的储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>oblivious</b></td><td>/əˈblɪviəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，不知情的</td><td>oblivious to the noise<br>察觉不到噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Deep in her book, she was oblivious to the noise of the crowd.  她沉浸在书里，察觉不到人群的嘈杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incognizant</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑɡnɪzənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未意识到的，不知道的</td><td>incognizant of the danger<br>未意识到危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swimmers were incognizant of the strong current below.  游泳者未意识到水下的强流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unaware</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈwer/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，未察觉的</td><td>be unaware of the rule<br>不知道这条规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were unaware that the meeting had been moved to Friday.  他们不知道会议已改到周五。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconscious</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːnʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意识的；昏迷的</td><td>an unconscious habit<br>无意识的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver lay unconscious on the road until help arrived.  司机躺在路上昏迷不醒，直到救援到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uninformed</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不了解情况的，无知的</td><td>an uninformed opinion<br>不明情况的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Uninformed criticism rarely helps anyone improve their work.  不了解情况的批评很少能帮助别人改进工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unwitting</td><td>/ʌnˈwɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，未觉察的</td><td>an unwitting accomplice<br>不知情的帮凶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became an unwitting participant in the whole scheme.  她不知不觉地成了整个阴谋的参与者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mindful</td><td>/ˈmaɪndfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>mindful of the risks<br>留意风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be mindful of the time when you answer each question.  回答每个问题时要留意时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigilant</td><td>/ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警醒的，警惕的</td><td>remain vigilant<br>保持警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards must remain vigilant throughout the long and quiet night shift.  守卫在漫长安静的夜班中必须始终保持警惕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquainted</td><td>/əˈkweɪntɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，认识的</td><td>be acquainted with<br>熟悉；与……相识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am not well acquainted with the rules of this game.  我对这个游戏的规则不太熟悉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cognizant</td><td>/ˈkɑːɡnɪzənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>知道的，意识到的</td><td>be cognizant of risks<br>意识到风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors must be cognizant of the risks before signing anything.  投资者在签署任何文件前必须意识到风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conscious</td><td>/ˈkɑːnʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有意识的，察觉到的</td><td>a conscious decision<br>有意识的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a conscious effort to speak more slowly on stage.  他有意识地努力在台上讲得慢一些。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conversant</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrsnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>conversant with the law<br>通晓法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fully conversant with the new safety regulations.  她非常熟悉新的安全条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grounded</td><td>/ˈɡraʊndɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>在地面上的，停飞的</td><td>grounded by the storm<br>因暴风雨而停飞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All flights were grounded because of the heavy fog.  由于大雾，所有航班都被迫停飞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 knowing</td><td>/ˈnoʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>故意的，明知的</td><td>a knowing violation of the rules<br>明知故犯的违规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge called it a knowing violation of the safety rules.  法官称这是明知故犯地违反安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 witting</td><td>/ˈwɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>知情的，有意的</td><td>a witting accomplice<br>知情的共犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a witting participant in the scheme from the start.  她从一开始就是这个计划的知情参与者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>obloquy</b></del></td><td>/ˈɑːbləkwi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谩骂，诽谤</td><td>suffer public obloquy<br>遭受公众谩骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The former minister suffered public obloquy for years after the scandal broke.  丑闻爆发后，这位前部长多年遭受公众谩骂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 billingsgate</td><td>/ˈbɪlɪŋzɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>粗话，脏话辱骂</td><td>a stream of billingsgate<br>一连串脏话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver let loose a stream of billingsgate at us.  那司机对我们大骂一连串脏话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fulmination</del></td><td>/ˌfʌlmɪˈneɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严厉谴责，痛斥</td><td>fulminations against the press<br>对媒体的痛斥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His nightly fulminations against the press amused nobody.  他每晚对媒体的痛斥没让谁觉得有趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invective</td><td>/ɪnˈvektɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>谩骂，痛骂；侮辱性的</td><td>a stream of invective<br>一连串的谩骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The politician's invective attacks on his rival shocked even his own supporters.  这位政客对对手的侮辱性攻击甚至震惊了他自己的支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scurrility</del></td><td>/skəˈrɪlɪti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶言辱骂，下流言语</td><td>descend into scurrility<br>沦为恶言辱骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate soon descended into open scurrility.  辩论很快沦为公开的恶言辱骂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitriol</td><td>/ˈvɪtriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>尖刻的批评，恶言</td><td>pour vitriol on someone<br>对某人恶言相加</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers poured vitriol on the director's second film.  评论者对导演的第二部片恶言相加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vituperation</td><td>/vaɪˌtupəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谩骂，苛责</td><td>a torrent of vituperation<br>一阵谩骂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She endured a torrent of vituperation without replying once.  她忍受一阵谩骂而一句未回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adulation</td><td>/ˌædʒəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>奉承，谄媚，过度崇拜</td><td>the adulation of fans<br>粉丝的狂热崇拜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young star grew uncomfortable with the constant adulation of crowds.  这位年轻明星对人群不断的追捧感到不适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obscure</b></td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambiguous</td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arcane</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的，难以理解的</td><td>arcane rules<br>晦涩难懂的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is full of arcane rules nobody understands.  税法充满无人理解的晦涩规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enigmatic</td><td>/ˌenɪɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>an enigmatic smile<br>神秘的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enigmatic answer left the reporters guessing for several days.  她神秘的回答让记者们猜了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fuliginous</del></td><td>/fjuˈlɪdʒɪnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煤烟色的，晦暗的</td><td>a fuliginous sky<br>烟黑的天空</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fuliginous haze hung over the factory district.  厂区上空笼罩着烟黑的雾霭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inscrutable</td><td>/ɪnˈskruːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高深莫测的，难以理解的</td><td>an inscrutable expression<br>高深莫测的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inscrutable smile gave nothing away about his plans.  他那高深莫测的笑容丝毫没有透露他的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opaque</td><td>/oʊˈpeɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不透明的；晦涩难懂的</td><td>opaque legal language<br>晦涩的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regulations remain opaque even to experienced tax lawyers.  这些法规连资深税务律师都觉得晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 blear</del></td><td>/blɪr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使模糊；朦胧的</td><td>blear the vision<br>使视线模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tears and smoke bleared his vision at the doorway.  眼泪和烟雾使他在门口视线模糊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blur</td><td>/blɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）变得朦胧，（使）变得不清楚；使不易理解</td><td>blur the distinction<br>模糊了界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New technology tends to blur the distinction between work and home.  新技术往往模糊了工作与家庭的界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 darken</td><td>/ˈdɑːrkən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使变暗，变黑</td><td>darken the room<br>使房间变暗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy clouds darkened the valley by mid-afternoon.  厚云在午后使山谷变暗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overshadow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈʃædoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使相形失色；使蒙上阴影</td><td>overshadow her earlier work<br>使她早期作品相形失色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her later novels overshadow the modest work that first made her famous.  她后来的小说使那部最初让她成名的平实作品相形失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shroud</td><td>/ʃraʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>将…从视线中隔离，遮蔽</td><td>shrouded in mist<br>笼罩在雾中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick mist shrouded the harbor and hid the ships from view.  浓雾笼罩港口，把船只从视线中遮住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 noteless</del></td><td>/ˈnoʊtləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不出名的，无人注意的</td><td>a noteless village<br>无名的村庄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was born in a noteless village near the border.  他生在边境附近一个无名村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uncelebrated</del></td><td>/ʌnˈsɛlɪbreɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受赞誉的，不出名的</td><td>an uncelebrated pioneer<br>未受赞誉的先驱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains an uncelebrated pioneer of early radio.  她仍是早期广播一位未受赞誉的先驱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unfamous</del></td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪməs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不出名的</td><td>an unfamous painter<br>不出名的画家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery deliberately shows work by unfamous painters.  这家画廊有意展出不出名画家的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unknown</td><td>/ˌʌnˈnoʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未知的，不为人知的</td><td>an unknown author<br>无名的作者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diary was written by an unknown author in 1830.  这本日记由一位无名作者于1830年写成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrecognized</td><td>/ˌʌnˈrekəɡnaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被认可的，未被认出的</td><td>unrecognized talent<br>未被认可的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His talent went unrecognized until long after his death.  他的才华直到死后很久才被认可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsung</td><td>/ˌʌnˈsʌŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被埋没的；不知名的</td><td>an unsung hero<br>无名英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unsung engineers behind the project never appeared in any press photo.  项目背后那些默默无闻的工程师从未出现在任何新闻照片里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explicit</td><td>/ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，明确的</td><td>explicit instructions<br>明确的指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave explicit instructions about how to store the samples.  她就样品的保存方式给出了明确指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 certain</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，无疑的</td><td>certain of the outcome<br>对结果有把握</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are certain that the letter was never sent.  我们确信那封信从未寄出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accessible</td><td>/əkˈsesəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可以到达的；可理解的</td><td>an accessible explanation<br>易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes physics accessible to ordinary readers.  他让普通读者也能理解物理学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonambiguous</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnæmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，无歧义的</td><td>nonambiguous wording<br>无歧义的措辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract needs nonambiguous wording about delivery dates.  合同需要关于交货日期的无歧义措辞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obvious</td><td>/ˈɑːbviəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显而易见的</td><td>an obvious mistake<br>明显的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The answer seemed obvious once she explained the method.  她解释了方法后，答案似乎显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unambiguous</td><td>/ˌʌnæmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，毫不含糊的</td><td>unambiguous written instructions<br>明确的书面指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract gives unambiguous rules about late payment.  合同就迟付款给出了明确规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unequivocal</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不含糊的，明确的</td><td>an unequivocal denial<br>毫不含糊的否认</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board issued an unequivocal statement supporting the director.  董事会发表了明确支持主任的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 legendary</td><td>/ˈledʒənˌderi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，传奇的</td><td>a legendary singer<br>传奇歌手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The legendary singer gave her last concert here.  那位传奇歌手在此举行了最后一场演唱会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 celebrated</td><td>/ˈseləˌbreɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，受赞誉的</td><td>a celebrated stage actor<br>著名的舞台演员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays work by several celebrated local painters.  博物馆展出了几位著名本地画家的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 famed</td><td>/ˈfeɪmd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闻名的，著名的</td><td>famed for her courage<br>以勇气闻名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley is famed for its cold, clear spring water.  这座山谷以清冷的泉水而闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 famous</td><td>/ˈfeɪməs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，出名的</td><td>famous for its seafood<br>以海鲜闻名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town is famous for its handmade paper.  这座小镇以手工纸而闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noted</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，知名的</td><td>a noted scholar<br>知名学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A noted scholar of Persian poetry opened the conference.  一位知名的波斯诗歌学者为会议开幕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 notorious</td><td>/noʊˈtɔːriəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>声名狼藉的</td><td>notorious for corruption<br>因腐败而臭名昭著</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighborhood was notorious for its late-night noise and crime.  这个街区因深夜噪音和犯罪而声名狼藉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prominent</td><td>/ˈprɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的；显眼的</td><td>a prominent local politician<br>当地知名的政治人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her name appears in a prominent place on the donor list.  她的名字出现在捐赠名单的显眼位置。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 renowned</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有名望的，著名的</td><td>renowned throughout Europe<br>闻名整个欧洲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city is renowned for its ancient bridges and narrow lanes.  这座城市以古桥和窄巷闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-known</td><td>/ˌwelˈnoʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>众所周知的，著名的</td><td>a well-known fact<br>众所周知的事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is a well-known fact that the bridge leaks.  这座桥漏水是众所周知的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 celebrity</td><td>/səˈlebrɪti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>名人；名声</td><td>a local television celebrity<br>当地的电视名人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restaurant is full of celebrities every Friday night.  每周五晚这家餐厅都挤满名人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obsequious</b></td><td>/əbˈsiːkwiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谄媚的，奴性的</td><td>obsequious flattery<br>谄媚的奉承</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter's obsequious flattery made the whole table distinctly uncomfortable.  侍者谄媚的奉承让整桌人都明显不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 supercilious</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsɪliəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高傲的，傲慢的</td><td>a supercilious smile<br>傲慢的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supercilious smile crossed his face when he heard my question.  听到我的问题，他脸上掠过傲慢的微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obsess</b></td><td>/əbˈses/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>迷住；使困扰</td><td>obsessed with detail<br>痴迷于细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was obsessed with detail and rewrote every paragraph at least four times.  他痴迷于细节，每段都至少重写四遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensively disgust</td><td>/ɪnˈtɛnsɪvli dɪsˈɡʌst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使强烈厌恶</td><td>intensively disgust the viewers<br>使观众强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scenes of cruelty intensively disgust most ordinary viewers.  残酷场面使多数普通观众强烈厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obstinate</b></td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intransigent</td><td>/ɪnˈtrænzɪdʒənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，固执的</td><td>an intransigent attitude<br>不妥协的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides remained intransigent, so the negotiations collapsed.  双方都毫不妥协，谈判因此破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncompromising</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmprəmaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，坚定的</td><td>an uncompromising stance<br>毫不妥协的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took an uncompromising stand against corruption in the department.  她对部门内的腐败采取了毫不妥协的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unyielding</td><td>/ʌnˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不屈的，不让步的</td><td>unyielding opposition<br>毫不让步的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union remained unyielding on the question of wages.  工会在工资问题上依然毫不让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquiescent</td><td>/ˌækwiˈesnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，默许的</td><td>an acquiescent audience<br>顺从的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too acquiescent to protest, the staff accepted the new rules.  员工太顺从，没有抗议就接受了新规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agreeable</td><td>/əˈɡriːəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉悦的；相一致的</td><td>an agreeable companion<br>一个讨人喜欢的同伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found him an agreeable companion on the long train journey.  她觉得他是长途火车旅程中一位讨人喜欢的同伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 complying</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，遵从的</td><td>complying with the rules<br>遵从规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firms complying with the standard receive a tax rebate.  遵从该标准的企业可获得退税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relenting</td><td>/rɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>态度软化的，让步的</td><td>show a relenting attitude<br>表现出软化的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of pleading, his relenting expression gave us hope.  在几小时的哀求之后，他态度软化的表情给了我们希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obstreperous</b></td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incompliant</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顺从的，不依从的</td><td>an incompliant witness<br>不肯配合的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incompliant staff refused to follow the new dress rule.  不顺从的员工拒绝遵守新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disciplined</td><td>/ˈdɪsəplənd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守纪律的，自律的</td><td>a disciplined class<br>守纪律的班级</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her disciplined class worked quietly without any supervision.  她那守纪律的班级无人监督也安静做事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 biddable</td><td>/ˈbɪdəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，听话的</td><td>a biddable pupil<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The biddable pupil did every task without complaint.  那听话的学生毫无抱怨地完成每项任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conformable</td><td>/kənˈfɔrməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；一致的</td><td>conformable to the rules<br>符合规定的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design must be conformable to the city's new safety code.  设计必须符合城市新的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ruly</del></td><td>/ˈruli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守规矩的，服管的</td><td>a ruly classroom<br>守规矩的教室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher was surprised to find such a ruly classroom.  新老师惊讶地发现这个教室如此守规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obstruct</b></td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fetter</td><td>/ˈfetər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚</td><td>the fetters of tradition<br>传统的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy family obligations fettered her ambition for many long years.  沉重的家庭责任多年来束缚了她的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handicap</td><td>/ˈhændikæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>妨碍，使不利；障碍</td><td>handicap the team<br>使球队处于不利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor equipment handicapped the researchers from the very start.  设备不足从一开始就妨碍了研究人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inhibit</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，阻碍</td><td>inhibit the growth of bacteria<br>抑制细菌生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold weather inhibits the growth of these tropical plants.  寒冷天气抑制了这些热带植物的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stymie</td><td>/ˈstaɪmi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>stymie an investigation<br>阻碍调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing records stymied the investigation for months.  档案缺失使调查停滞了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interfere with</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfɪr wɪð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>干扰，妨碍</td><td>interfere with the work<br>妨碍工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud noise from the street interferes with my concentration.  街上的噪音干扰我集中注意力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facilitate</td><td>/fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变容易，促进</td><td>facilitate the discussion<br>促进讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new airport will facilitate tourism throughout the whole region.  新机场将促进整个地区的旅游业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aid</td><td>/ˈeɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>come to someone's aid<br>前来帮助某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came to her aid within minutes of the fire.  火灾发生几分钟内邻居们就来帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obtuse</b></td><td>/əbˈtuːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚钝的</td><td>deliberately obtuse answers<br>故意装糊涂的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave deliberately obtuse answers to every question the inspector asked him.  他对检查员问的每个问题都故意给出装糊涂的回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dumb</td><td>/dʌm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚蠢的；不会说话的</td><td>a dumb question<br>愚蠢的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking that in public was a pretty dumb idea.  在公开场合问那个是个相当愚蠢的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fatuous</td><td>/ˈfætʃuəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚笨的，昏庸的</td><td>a fatuous remark<br>愚蠢的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fatuous comment showed he had not read the report.  他愚蠢的评论说明他没读过那份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mindless</td><td>/ˈmaɪndləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不需用脑的；愚蠢的</td><td>mindless routine work<br>不需用脑的例行工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted mindless work that let him rest his brain.  他想做点不用脑的活儿，让大脑休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oafish</td><td>/ˈoʊfɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚笨粗鲁的</td><td>oafish manners<br>粗笨的举止</td></tr>
