<tr><td colspan="7">例 A general gloominess settled over the town that winter.  那年冬天，一片阴郁笼罩着小镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>paradox</b></td><td>/ˈpærədɑːks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>矛盾，悖论</td><td>an apparent paradox<br>表面上的矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is a paradox that the richest country has so many hungry children.  最富有的国家却有这么多饥饿的孩子，这是个悖论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dichotomy</td><td>/daɪˈkɑːtəmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对立</td><td>a false dichotomy<br>虚假的二分对立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book rejects the simple dichotomy between nature and human culture.  这本书否认自然与人类文化之间的简单对立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incongruity</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈɡruːəti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不协调，不一致</td><td>a glaring incongruity<br>明显的不协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The incongruity between his words and his actions puzzled everyone.  他言行之间的不一致让所有人困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>paragon</b></td><td>/ˈpærəɡɑːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>优秀模范；把…比作；显示相似</td><td>a paragon of patience<br>耐心的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old librarian was a paragon of patience with every noisy student.  那位老图书馆员对每个吵闹的学生都是耐心的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archetype</td><td>/ˈɑːrkɪtaɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原型，典型</td><td>the archetype of a hero<br>英雄的典型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sherlock Holmes remains the archetype of the brilliant private detective.  夏洛克·福尔摩斯仍是天才私家侦探的原型。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 example</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæmpəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>范例，典范；例子</td><td>set a good example<br>作出好榜样</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chapel is a fine example of early Gothic building.  这座礼拜堂是早期哥特建筑的绝佳范例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exemplar</td><td>/ɪɡˈzemplɑr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>典范，模范</td><td>an exemplar of restraint<br>克制的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her career remains an exemplar of patient scientific work.  她的生涯至今仍是耐心科研的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ideal</td><td>/aɪˈdiːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>理想，典范；理想的</td><td>an ideal solution<br>理想的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains the ideal of quiet courage for many students.  她仍是许多学生心中沉静勇气的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 model</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>典范，模范；模型</td><td>a model of efficiency<br>效率的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic became a model of efficiency for the region.  这家诊所成了全区效率的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pattern</td><td>/ˈpætərn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>范例，样板；模式</td><td>a pattern to follow<br>可效法的样板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform became a pattern for other cities to follow.  这项改革成了其他城市效法的样板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paradigm</td><td>/ˈpærədaɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>典范，模范</td><td>a paradigm of good design<br>优秀设计的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This small library is a paradigm of thoughtful modern design.  这座小图书馆是精心现代设计的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assimilate</td><td>/əˈsɪməˌleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…比拟为；吸收，同化</td><td>assimilate one case to another<br>把一个案例比拟另一个</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics assimilate her early work to French realism.  批评家把她的早期作品比作法国现实主义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compare</td><td>/kəmˈper/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>比较，比作</td><td>compare it to a garden<br>把它比作一座花园</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet compared memory to a house with locked rooms.  诗人把记忆比作有锁着房间的屋子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equate</td><td>/ɪˈkweɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…等同看待</td><td>equate wealth with success<br>把财富等同于成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not equate silence with agreement.  不要把沉默等同于同意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liken</td><td>/ˈlaɪkən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…比作，比拟</td><td>liken life to a journey<br>把人生比作旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poets often liken life to a long and uncertain journey.  诗人常把生活比作一场漫长而不确定的旅程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 match</td><td>/ˈmætʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>与…相配，比得上</td><td>match his rival's skill<br>比得上对手的技艺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few players could match his skill on wet grass.  湿草地上少有球员比得上他的技艺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parallel</td><td>/ˈperəˌlel/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>相似的，对应的；平行的</td><td>a parallel case<br>类似的案例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians found a parallel case in a much earlier war.  历史学家在一场更早的战争中找到了类似的例子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contrast</td><td>/ˈkɑːntræst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>差异，对比；对照</td><td>a sharp contrast<br>鲜明的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a sharp contrast between his words and his actions.  他的言语与行为之间有鲜明的差异。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>paramount</b></td><td>/ˈpærəmaʊnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>最重要的</td><td>of paramount importance<br>至为重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety is paramount when workers handle these highly flammable chemicals.  工人处理这些极易燃的化学品时，安全最为重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cardinal</td><td>/ˈkɑːrdənl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，非常重要的</td><td>a cardinal rule<br>一条首要的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Honesty is a cardinal rule in this small hospital.  诚实是这家小医院的首要准则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chief</td><td>/ˈtʃiːf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；首领</td><td>the chief reason<br>主要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was the chief reason they abandoned the project.  成本是他们放弃这个项目的主要原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 key</td><td>/ˈkiː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>关键的；钥匙</td><td>a key factor<br>关键因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is the key element in any long working relationship.  信任是任何长期工作关系中的关键要素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leading</td><td>/ˈliːdɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，领先的</td><td>a leading expert<br>顶尖专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a leading authority on ancient Chinese bronzes.  她是中国古代青铜器的顶尖权威。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 main</td><td>/ˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，最重要的</td><td>the main point<br>主要论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The main problem is that we have almost no time.  主要问题是我们几乎没有时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predominant</td><td>/prɪˈdɑːmənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，占优势的</td><td>the predominant view<br>占主导的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rice remains the predominant crop in the southern valleys.  稻米仍是南部谷地的主要作物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preeminent</td><td>/priˈemɪnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>优秀的，重要的</td><td>the preeminent scholar<br>最杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is the preeminent authority on medieval trade routes in Europe.  他是研究欧洲中世纪贸易路线最杰出的权威。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 primary</td><td>/ˈpraɪˌmeri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>首要的，主要的</td><td>the primary concern<br>首要的关切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety was the primary concern in redesigning the bridge.  重新设计桥梁时安全是首要关切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 principal</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；校长</td><td>the principal cause<br>首要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourism is the principal source of income on the island.  旅游业是这个岛上的主要收入来源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supreme</td><td>/səˈpriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>最高的，至上的</td><td>of supreme importance<br>极其重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court holds supreme authority over all such disputes.  该法院对所有此类争议拥有最高裁判权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ancillary</td><td>/ˈænsəleri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>次要的；辅助的，补充的</td><td>ancillary services<br>辅助性服务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hospital also provides ancillary services such as laundry and catering.  医院还提供洗衣和餐饮等辅助性服务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 secondary</td><td>/ˈsekənˌderi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>次要的，从属的</td><td>of secondary importance<br>重要性次之</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was secondary to safety in the final decision.  在最终决定中，成本次于安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>paranoid</b></td><td>/ˈpærənɔɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多疑的，对他人极端恐惧和怀疑的</td><td>paranoid about being watched<br>疑心被人监视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew paranoid, convinced that his neighbors were reading his mail.  他变得多疑，确信邻居在偷看他的信件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distrustful</td><td>/dɪsˈtrʌstfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不信任的，怀疑的</td><td>distrustful of officials<br>不信任官员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters remain distrustful of every promise made.  选民对所做的每个承诺仍不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nervous</td><td>/ˈnɜːrvəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧张不安的，忧虑的</td><td>nervous about the results<br>对结果感到不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew nervous about the results as the week passed.  一周过去，她对结果越来越不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspicious</td><td>/səˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怀疑的；可疑的</td><td>suspicious of his motives<br>怀疑他的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard grew suspicious of the unmarked van.  保安对那辆无标识的货车起了疑心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbelieving</td><td>/ˌʌnbəˈliːvɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相信的，怀疑的</td><td>an unbelieving stare<br>怀疑的注视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He met the explanation with an unbelieving stare.  他用怀疑的目光回应这个解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worried</td><td>/ˈwɜːrid/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>担心的，忧虑的</td><td>worried about the debt<br>为债务担忧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parents were worried about the flooded road to school.  家长为通往学校的淹水路面担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 credulous</td><td>/ˈkredʒələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻信的，易受骗的</td><td>a credulous audience<br>轻信的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement was designed to fool credulous shoppers with fake reviews.  这则广告用虚假评论来欺骗轻信的购物者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>paraphrase</b></td><td>/ˈpærəfreɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>转述，意译，改写</td><td>paraphrase the argument<br>转述该论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please paraphrase the main argument in two or three simple sentences.  请用两三句简单的话转述主要论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rephrase</td><td>/riˈfreɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>换个说法，重新表述</td><td>rephrase the question<br>换个说法提问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let me rephrase the question in simpler terms.  让我用更简单的说法重问一遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restate</td><td>/riˈsteɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重申，重新表述</td><td>restate the main point<br>重申要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She restated the main point so everyone could follow.  她重述了要点，让大家都跟得上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reword</td><td>/riˈwɜːrd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改换措辞，重写</td><td>reword the contract clause<br>改写合同条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyers carefully reworded the clause about late payment.  律师们仔细改写了关于逾期付款的条款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 translate</td><td>/trænzˈleɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>翻译；转化</td><td>translate the poem loosely<br>意译这首诗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He translated the letters from Russian into plain English.  他把这些信从俄语译成平实的英语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quote</td><td>/ˈkwoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>引用，引述</td><td>quote the passage exactly<br>原文引用该段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked her to quote the passage exactly.  编辑要求她一字不差地引用该段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>parch</b></del></td><td>/pɑːrtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>烤干，使干透</td><td>parch the dry earth<br>把干土烤裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks without rain had parched the fields into cracked brown dust.  数周无雨已把田地烤得干裂成褐色的尘土。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dehydrate</td><td>/diːˈhaɪdreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>去除水分，使干燥；使失去活力</td><td>dehydrate fruit for storage<br>把水果脱水储存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Campers dehydrate fruit and meat so the food lasts for weeks.  露营者把水果和肉脱水，好让食物保存数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 desiccate</del></td><td>/ˈdesɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使干燥，使脱水</td><td>desiccate the soil completely<br>使土壤完全干透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without rain desiccated the fields and killed the young corn.  数月无雨使田地干透，幼小的玉米也枯死了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dry</td><td>/ˈdraɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干的，干燥的；使干燥</td><td>bone dry soil<br>干透的土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hang the towels outside until they are completely dry.  把毛巾挂到外面直到完全干燥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scorch</td><td>/skɔːrtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>炙烤，烘干</td><td>scorch the grass brown<br>把草烤成褐色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blazing sun scorched the grass brown within a single week.  烈日一周之内就把草烤成褐色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sear</td><td>/ˈsɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>烤焦，烧灼；使枯萎</td><td>sear the meat quickly<br>快速把肉煎焦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sear the meat quickly on both sides before roasting it.  烤之前先把肉两面快速煎焦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to douse the flames on the upper floors.  消防员整夜奋战，扑灭了高层的火焰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 drench</td><td>/drentʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使湿透，浸透</td><td>drenched to the skin<br>浑身湿透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden storm drenched us to the skin before we reached shelter.  一场突来的暴雨在我们找到避处前就把我们浇得浑身湿透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hydrate</td><td>/ˈhaɪˌdreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使含水，补水</td><td>hydrate the skin thoroughly<br>给皮肤充分补水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drink water regularly to hydrate your body during long runs.  长跑时要定时喝水给身体补水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wash</td><td>/ˈwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>冲洗，洗涤</td><td>wash away the dirt<br>冲掉污垢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain washed away the dirt from the steps.  大雨把台阶上的污垢冲掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 water</td><td>/ˈwɔːtər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>给…浇水，灌溉</td><td>water the garden<br>给花园浇水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waters the vegetable beds every morning before sunrise.  他每天日出前给菜地浇水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wet</td><td>/ˈwet/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>湿的，潮的；弄湿</td><td>soaking wet clothes<br>湿透的衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My shoes got wet crossing the flooded street.  我的鞋在穿过被淹的街道时湿了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parenthesis</b></td><td>/pəˈrenθəsɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间断</td><td>a brief parenthesis<br>短暂的间断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war was only a brief parenthesis in the peaceful history of the town.  那场战争只是这个小镇平静历史中一段短暂的间断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discontinuity</td><td>/dɪˌskɑːntɪˈnuːɪti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间断，中断，不连贯</td><td>a discontinuity in the record<br>记录中的一处断裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Geologists found a sharp discontinuity in the rock layers.  地质学家在岩层中发现一处明显的断裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interim</td><td>/ˈɪntərɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>中间过渡时期，间隔；暂时的</td><td>an interim report<br>中期报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee published an interim report while the investigation was still continuing.  委员会在调查仍在进行时发布了一份中期报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interlude</td><td>/ˈɪntərluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>间隙，插曲</td><td>a brief interlude<br>短暂的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trip was a pleasant interlude in a very busy year.  这次旅行是忙碌一年中愉快的插曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intermission</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>中场休息，间歇</td><td>during the intermission<br>在中场休息时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We bought coffee during the long intermission.  漫长的中场休息时我们买了咖啡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interregnum</td><td>/ˌɪntəˈreɡnəm/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>过渡期</td><td>a brief interregnum<br>短暂的过渡期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief interregnum followed the old king's death before his nephew arrived.  老国王去世后出现了一段短暂的政权空缺期，直到他侄子到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interruption</td><td>/ˌɪntəˈrʌpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>中断，打扰</td><td>without interruption<br>不受打扰地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She worked for three hours without a single interruption.  她连续工作三小时未被打扰一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 interstice</del></td><td>/ɪnˈtɜrstɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缝隙，间隙</td><td>interstices of the wall<br>墙上的缝隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Moss grew in the interstices of the old stone wall.  苔藓长在老石墙的缝隙里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interval</td><td>/ˈɪntərvəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>间隔，间歇</td><td>at regular intervals<br>每隔一定时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buses arrive at ten-minute intervals during rush hour.  高峰时段公交每隔十分钟一班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuation</td><td>/kənˌtɪnjuˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>继续，延续</td><td>the continuation of talks<br>会谈的继续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The continuation of the project depends on further funding.  项目的继续取决于后续资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuity</td><td>/ˌkɑːntəˈnuːəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>连续性，持续</td><td>ensure continuity of service<br>保证服务的连续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The handover plan preserves continuity in patient care.  交接方案保证了病患护理的连续性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pariah</b></td><td>/pəˈraɪə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>被排斥或鄙视的人</td><td>become a social pariah<br>成为被社会排斥的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the scandal he became a pariah among his former colleagues.  丑闻之后，他成了前同事中被排斥的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castaway</td><td>/ˈkæstəˌweɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>被抛弃者；漂流者</td><td>a lonely castaway<br>孤独的漂流者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel follows a castaway on an empty coral island.  小说讲述一名漂流者在无人珊瑚岛上的经历。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castoff</td><td>/ˈkæstˌɔːf/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>被抛弃者；丢弃之物</td><td>society's castoffs<br>被社会抛弃的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shelter took in castoffs whom no one else wanted.  这家收容所接纳了无人愿要的被弃者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leper</td><td>/ˈlepər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>被众人回避的人；麻风病人</td><td>treated like a leper<br>被当作瘟神般回避</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the scandal he was treated like a leper at work.  丑闻之后他在单位被当作瘟神般回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respectable person</td><td>/rɪˈspɛktəbəl ˈpɜrsən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>受人尊敬的体面人</td><td>a thoroughly respectable person<br>十足体面的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors described him as a quiet, respectable person.  邻居们说他是个安静而体面的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parity</b></td><td>/ˈpærəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>相称，同等，平等</td><td>achieve parity in pay<br>实现薪酬平等</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union fought for years to achieve parity in pay between both branches.  工会奋斗多年，以实现两个分部之间的薪酬平等。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coequality</del></td><td>/ˌkoʊɪˈkwɑlɪti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>完全平等，同等地位</td><td>the coequality of branches<br>各部门的平等地位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charter insists on the coequality of all member states.  该章程坚持所有成员国地位完全平等。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coordinateness</del></td><td>/koʊˈɔrdɪnətnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同等地位，并列关系</td><td>coordinateness of two clauses<br>两分句的并列</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grammarians stress the coordinateness of these two clauses.  语法学家强调这两个分句的并列关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equality</td><td>/ɪˈkwɑːləti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>平等，同等</td><td>equality before the law<br>法律面前的平等</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movement fought for equality before the law.  这场运动争取法律面前的平等。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivalency</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvələnsi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>等同，等值</td><td>a degree equivalency<br>学历等值认定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The college granted her an equivalency for foreign credits.  学院对她的国外学分给予等值认定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 par</td><td>/ˈpɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>同等水平，标准</td><td>on a par with rivals<br>与对手不分高下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their new engine is on a par with the German design.  他们的新引擎与德国设计不分高下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sameness</td><td>/ˈseɪmnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>相同，一致；单调</td><td>a dull sameness<br>沉闷的雷同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dull sameness marked every house on the street.  街上每栋房子都是沉闷的雷同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disparity</td><td>/dɪˈspærəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>悬殊差异，不一致</td><td>income disparity<br>收入差距</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report highlights a growing disparity between the two regions.  报告强调两个地区之间日益扩大的差距。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 imparity</del></td><td>/ɪmˈpærɪti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不平等，不对等</td><td>imparity of treatment<br>待遇的不对等</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report exposed a clear imparity of treatment between branches.  报告揭露了各分部间明显的待遇不对等。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inequality</td><td>/ˌɪnɪˈkwɑːləti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不平等，不均等</td><td>growing income inequality<br>日益扩大的收入不平等</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Growing income inequality worried even the bank's economists.  日益扩大的收入不平等甚至令银行经济学家担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parody</b></td><td>/ˈpærədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（以嘲笑原作作者的）模仿作品，恶搞</td><td>a parody of the film<br>对该片的恶搞模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students wrote a hilarious parody of the morning speech at assembly.  学生们写了一篇滑稽的作品，恶搞集会上的早间讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burlesque</td><td>/bɜːrˈlesk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>滑稽夸张的模仿讽刺作品；恶搞</td><td>a burlesque of politics<br>对政治的滑稽恶搞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play is a clever burlesque of modern political debate.  这部戏是对当代政治辩论的巧妙滑稽模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caricature</td><td>/ˈkærɪkətʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>讽刺画，夸张的滑稽模仿</td><td>draw a caricature<br>画一幅讽刺画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cartoonist drew a cruel caricature of the new mayor.  漫画家画了一幅刻薄的新市长讽刺画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spoof</td><td>/spuːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松幽默的模仿，小恶搞</td><td>a spoof of the news<br>对新闻的幽默模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The show aired a clever spoof of the evening news broadcast.  节目播出了对晚间新闻的一段巧妙恶搞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travesty</td><td>/ˈtrævəsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拙劣的、嘲弄性模仿</td><td>a travesty of justice<br>对正义的拙劣讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the hasty trial a travesty of justice.  批评者称这场匆忙的审判是对正义的拙劣讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imitate</td><td>/ˈɪməˌteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>模仿，仿效</td><td>imitate his accent<br>模仿他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children naturally imitate the speech of adults around them.  孩子会自然地模仿身边大人的说话方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mock</td><td>/ˈmɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>假的，模拟的；嘲笑</td><td>a mock trial<br>模拟审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students held a mock election to learn how voting works.  学生举行模拟选举以了解投票如何运作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mimic</td><td>/ˈmɪmɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>模仿，仿效；仿造的</td><td>mimic human speech<br>模仿人类说话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain parrots can mimic human speech well enough to fool a casual listener.  某些鹦鹉能模仿人类说话，足以骗过不留意的听者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>paroxysm</b></td><td>/ˈpærəksɪzəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（政治、社会领域的）大动荡</td><td>a paroxysm of unrest<br>一场动荡的爆发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The assassination touched off a paroxysm of unrest across the whole region.  那场刺杀在整个地区引发了一场动荡的爆发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bouleversement</del></td><td>/ˌbulvɛrsəˈmɑn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剧变，彻底动荡</td><td>a political bouleversement<br>一场政治剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The election produced a bouleversement no analyst had predicted.  这次选举造成了无人预料的政治剧变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cataclysm</td><td>/ˈkætəklɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大灾难；剧变</td><td>a social cataclysm<br>社会剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eruption was a cataclysm that reshaped the entire island.  那次喷发是一场重塑整座岛屿的大灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 earthquake</td><td>/ˈɜːrθˌkweɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>剧烈动荡；地震</td><td>a political earthquake<br>政治地震</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The verdict was a political earthquake for the ruling party.  这项判决对执政党是一场政治地震。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurricane</td><td>/ˈhɜːrəkeɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>飓风般的事物，引起动荡的事物</td><td>a hurricane of protest<br>抗议的风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A hurricane of protest followed the sudden announcement of the new tax.  新税突然宣布之后，随即掀起一场抗议的风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 storm</td><td>/ˈstɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暴风雨；一阵猛烈（如抗议）</td><td>a storm of protest<br>一阵抗议风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision caused a storm of protest on social media.  这一决定在社交媒体上引发抗议风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempest</td><td>/ˈtempəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>风暴；骚动，风波</td><td>a tempest of protest<br>抗议的风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark raised a tempest of protest online.  他随意的一句话在网上掀起抗议风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tumult</td><td>/ˈtuːmʌlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚动，暴动</td><td>the tumult of the crowd<br>人群的骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tumult in the square grew louder as more protesters arrived.  随着更多抗议者到来，广场上的骚动越来越大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upheaval</td><td>/əpˈhiːvəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>剧变，动乱</td><td>social upheaval<br>社会剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war brought decades of social upheaval to the region.  战争给这一地区带来数十年的社会剧变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parrot</b></td><td>/ˈpærət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（机械地）模仿，复制</td><td>parrot the official line<br>机械复述官方说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students merely parrot the textbook instead of forming their own opinions.  学生们只是机械复述课本，而不形成自己的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copy</td><td>/ˈkɑːpi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品，仿制品；抄写</td><td>a cheap copy<br>廉价仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting on the wall is only a cheap copy.  墙上那幅画只是廉价的仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ditto</td><td>/ˈdɪtoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>重复，照样再来；同上</td><td>ditto the previous answer<br>照抄上一个答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The junior members simply dittoed whatever the chairman said.  年轻成员只是照搬主席说的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 duplicate</td><td>/ˈduːpləkət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品</td><td>an exact duplicate<br>完全一样的副本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep an exact duplicate of the contract in a separate locked drawer.  把合同的一份完全一样的副本另放在一个上锁的抽屉里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 echo</td><td>/ˈekoʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>附和，重复；回声</td><td>echo his opinion<br>附和他的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Younger delegates simply echoed the governor's opinion.  年轻代表只是附和州长的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quote</td><td>/ˈkwoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>引用，引述</td><td>quote the passage exactly<br>原文引用该段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked her to quote the passage exactly.  编辑要求她一字不差地引用该段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coin</td><td>/ˈkɔɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>创造（新词）；硬币</td><td>coin a phrase<br>创造一个说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists coined the term to describe the new particle.  科学家创造了这个术语来描述这种新粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 create</td><td>/kriˈeɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>创造，创立</td><td>create a new department<br>创建新部门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city created a fund to support local artists.  该市设立了一项基金支持本地艺术家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 devise</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想出，设计，策划</td><td>devise a plan<br>想出一个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team devised a clever method for testing the old samples.  团队想出了检验旧样本的巧妙方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invent</td><td>/ˌɪnˈvent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>发明；编造</td><td>invent an excuse<br>编造借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He invented a clumsy excuse for missing the morning meeting.  他为缺席早会编了个拙劣的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parry</b></td><td>/ˈpæri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲避，回避（问题）</td><td>parry the question<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator tried to parry every question about his campaign funds.  那位参议员试图回避每一个关于竞选资金的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avoid</td><td>/əˈvɔɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避免，回避</td><td>avoid the issue<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He carefully avoided answering the reporter's most difficult question.  他小心地避开了记者最难的那个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dodge</td><td>/dɑːdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>躲避</td><td>dodge the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister managed to dodge the question three times in one interview.  部长在一次采访中三次成功回避了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elude</td><td>/ɪˈluːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲闪，躲避；使无法理解，使困惑</td><td>elude the police<br>躲开警察</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief eluded the police for nearly six months before being caught.  小偷躲开警方近六个月才被抓住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eschew</td><td>/ɪsˈtʃuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刻意避开；戒绝</td><td>eschew violence<br>戒绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks eschew luxury and live on whatever the village offers.  僧侣们避开奢华，靠村民所给的一切生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evade</td><td>/ɪˈveɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>evade the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness evaded every question about the missing money.  证人回避了所有关于失踪钱款的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finesse</td><td>/fɪˈnes/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>娴熟技巧；（巧妙地）躲避</td><td>handle it with finesse<br>娴熟巧妙地处理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the awkward question with real finesse and everyone relaxed.  她娴熟巧妙地处理了那个尴尬的问题，大家都放松了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scape</td><td>/ˈskeɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>逃脱，避开</td><td>scape the worst of it<br>躲过最糟的部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village scaped the worst of the flood by sheer luck.  这个村子纯凭运气躲过了洪水最猛的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shirk</td><td>/ʃɜːrk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>shirk responsibility<br>逃避责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one on this team is allowed to shirk hard work.  这个团队里没人可以逃避苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shun</td><td>/ʃʌn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避开，避免</td><td>shun public attention<br>避开公众关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The writer chose to shun public attention after winning the prize.  那位作家获奖后选择避开公众关注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confront</td><td>/kənˈfrʌnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>直接对抗，直面</td><td>confront the problem<br>直面问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later you must confront the problem directly.  你早晚必须直面这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 face</td><td>/ˈfeɪs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>面对，正视；脸，面</td><td>face the truth<br>正视事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later she must face the truth about the debt.  她迟早得正视这笔债务的真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meet</td><td>/miːt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的</td><td>a meet response<br>合适的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A public apology seemed the only meet response to such a serious blunder.  公开道歉似乎是对如此严重失误唯一合适的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>parsimonious</b></td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 closefisted</del></td><td>/ˌkloʊsˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，一毛不拔的</td><td>a closefisted landlord<br>吝啬的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their closefisted landlord refused to fix the heater.  他们吝啬的房东拒绝修暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mean</td><td>/miːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的；吝啬的</td><td>a mean little room<br>简陋卑贱的小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family lived in a mean little room above a noisy printing shop.  一家人住在嘈杂印刷店楼上一间简陋的小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 mingy</del></td><td>/ˈmɪndʒi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的；少得可怜的</td><td>a mingy tip<br>少得可怜的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a mingy tip after a very long meal.  吃了很久之后他只留下少得可怜的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miserly</td><td>/ˈmaɪzərli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a miserly tip<br>吝啬的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a miserly tip after complaining about the service all evening.  他整晚抱怨服务，最后只留下吝啬的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 niggardly</td><td>/ˈnɪɡərdli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，少得可怜的</td><td>a niggardly raise<br>少得可怜的加薪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered a niggardly raise after record profits.  在创纪录盈利后公司给了少得可怜的加薪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 penurious</del></td><td>/pəˈnʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；吝啬的</td><td>penurious circumstances<br>贫困的境况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent a penurious youth in a crowded tenement.  他在拥挤的廉租房里度过了贫困的青年时期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 stinting</del></td><td>/ˈstɪntɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，节省克扣的</td><td>stinting with praise<br>吝于赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was stinting with praise but generous with advice.  他吝于赞扬，却乐于给建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tight</td><td>/ˈtaɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的；紧的</td><td>tight with money<br>在钱上很抠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her uncle was famously tight with money all his life.  她舅舅一生在钱上出名地抠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tightfisted</del></td><td>/ˌtaɪtˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a tightfisted employer<br>吝啬的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tightfisted employer counted every paper clip.  他们吝啬的雇主连回形针都要数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncharitable</td><td>/ʌnˈtʃɛrɪtəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不宽厚的，苛刻无情的</td><td>an uncharitable judgment<br>苛刻无情的评判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It would be uncharitable to blame her for the delay.  为这次延误责怪她就太苛刻了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ungenerous</td><td>/ʌnˈdʒɛnərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，不宽厚的</td><td>an ungenerous offer<br>不厚道的出价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed ungenerous to refuse her such a small favor.  拒绝她这么小的请求显得很不厚道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 generous</td><td>/ˈdʒenərəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的；慷慨的</td><td>a generous helping<br>满满的一份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She poured a generous glass of wine for each guest.  她给每位客人倒了满满一杯酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 munificent</td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的</td><td>a munificent donation<br>一笔慷慨的捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university received a munificent donation from a former engineering student.  这所大学收到了一位工程系校友的慷慨捐赠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>partial</b></td><td>/ˈpɑːrʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偏袒的，偏爱的；部分的，不完整的</td><td>partial to his friends<br>偏袒他的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was clearly partial to the home team throughout the match.  整场比赛裁判明显偏袒主队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 biased</td><td>/ˈbaɪəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，偏向的</td><td>a biased report<br>有偏见的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers complained that the coverage was clearly biased.  读者抱怨这些报道明显带有偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 one-sided</td><td>/ˌwʌnˈsaɪdɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>片面的，偏向一方的</td><td>a one-sided report<br>片面的报道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate felt one-sided because only supporters were invited.  这场辩论显得片面，因为只邀请了支持者。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partisan</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəzn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>党派偏见的；盲目拥护者</td><td>partisan politics<br>党派偏见的政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate quickly sank into partisan shouting about very old grievances.  辩论很快沦为带党派偏见的叫嚷，纠缠陈年旧怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prejudiced</td><td>/ˈpredʒədəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有偏见的，成见的</td><td>prejudiced against outsiders<br>对外来者有偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge dismissed the prejudiced juror before the trial began.  法官在审判开始前请走了那位有偏见的陪审员。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 deficient</td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>deficient in vitamins<br>缺乏维生素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diet was deficient in iron and essential vitamins.  这种饮食缺乏铁和必需的维生素。</td></tr>
</tbody>

<tr><td><del>↳近 fragmental</del></td><td>/fræɡˈmɛntəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>碎片的，不完整的</td><td>fragmental evidence<br>零碎的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only fragmental evidence of the settlement has survived.  该聚落只留下零碎的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fragmentary</td><td>/ˈfræɡmənˌteri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>零碎的，不完整的</td><td>fragmentary records<br>残缺不全的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our knowledge of his early years remains fragmentary.  我们对他早年的了解仍是零碎的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 half</td><td>/ˈhæf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>一半的；不完全的</td><td>a half measure<br>不彻底的办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half measures will not fix a bridge this badly cracked.  半吊子办法修不好裂得这么厉害的桥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 halfway</td><td>/ˈhæfweɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>半途的，不彻底的</td><td>a halfway solution<br>不彻底的方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They settled on a halfway solution that pleased nobody.  他们采取了一个谁都不满意的折中方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disinterested</td><td>/dɪsˈɪntrəstɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，无偏见的</td><td>a disinterested judgment<br>公正的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a disinterested judgment from someone with no stake in the outcome.  我们需要一个与结果无涉的人作出公正的判断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 equitable</td><td>/ˈekwətəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公平的，公正的</td><td>an equitable solution<br>公平的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They finally reached an equitable division of the property.  他们最终达成了对财产的公平分配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evenhanded</td><td>/ˌiːvənˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公平的，不偏袒的</td><td>evenhanded treatment<br>公平的对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee was praised for her evenhanded handling of the match.  裁判因公平处理这场比赛而受到称赞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fair</td><td>/ˈfer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的；相当的；晴朗的</td><td>a fair trial<br>公正的审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone deserves a fair hearing before any decision is made.  在做出任何决定之前，人人都应得到公正的申辩机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>

<tr><td>↳反 impartial</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːrʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的，不偏不倚的</td><td>an impartial observer<br>公正的观察者</td></tr>

</tbody>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful revision is an integral part of writing any serious research paper.  仔细修改是撰写任何严肃研究论文不可缺少的部分。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 whole</td><td>/ˈhoʊl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>整个的，完整的</td><td>the whole truth<br>全部真相</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parody</td><td>/ˈpærədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（以嘲笑原作作者的）模仿作品，恶搞</td><td>a parody of the film<br>对该片的恶搞模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students wrote a hilarious parody of the morning speech at assembly.  学生们写了一篇滑稽的作品，恶搞集会上的早间讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spoof</td><td>/spuːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松幽默的模仿，小恶搞</td><td>a spoof of the news<br>对新闻的幽默模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The show aired a clever spoof of the evening news broadcast.  节目播出了对晚间新闻的一段巧妙恶搞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travesty</td><td>/ˈtrævəsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拙劣的、嘲弄性模仿</td><td>a travesty of justice<br>对正义的拙劣讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the hasty trial a travesty of justice.  批评者称这场匆忙的审判是对正义的拙劣讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agglomerate</td><td>/əˈɡlɑːməreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结块，聚成一团</td><td>agglomerate into lumps<br>聚结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wet powder agglomerated into hard grey lumps.  湿粉结成了灰色的硬块。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collage</td><td>/kəˈlɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拼贴画，混合物</td><td>a collage of photographs<br>照片拼贴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her film is a collage of interviews and old home movies.  她的影片是访谈与旧家庭录像的拼贴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hodgepodge</td><td>/ˈhɑːdʒpɑːdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a hodgepodge of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The house is a hodgepodge of every style he admired.  这房子是他欣赏过的各种风格的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jumble</td><td>/ˈdʒʌmbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>混乱，杂乱堆积；使混乱</td><td>a jumble of ideas<br>杂乱的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her drawer held a jumble of receipts, coins, and old keys.  她的抽屉里杂乱地堆着收据、硬币和旧钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jungle</td><td>/ˈdʒʌŋɡəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>杂乱混合体；丛林</td><td>a jungle of regulations<br>杂乱如林的规章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Applicants face a jungle of overlapping regulations.  申请人面对的是一堆杂乱重叠的规章。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 medley</td><td>/ˈmedli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物</td><td>a medley of flavors<br>各种风味的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The buffet table offered a medley of flavors from six different countries.  自助餐台上供应着来自六个不同国家的各式风味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 montage</td><td>/mɑːnˈtɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a montage of images<br>图像的拼贴组合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibit opened with a montage of images from a century of protest.  展览以一组来自百年抗争的图像拼贴开场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motley</td><td>/ˈmɑːtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混杂的，富于变化的</td><td>a motley crowd<br>混杂的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motley crowd of farmers, students, and shopkeepers filled the town square.  农民、学生和店主组成的混杂人群挤满了市镇广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salad</td><td>/ˈsæləd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物；色拉</td><td>a salad of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The album is a salad of jazz, rock and folk tunes.  这张专辑是爵士、摇滚和民谣的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variety</td><td>/vəˈraɪəti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样，种类繁多</td><td>a wide variety of dishes<br>各式各样的菜肴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offers a wide variety of local cheeses.  市场上有各式各样的本地奶酪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 welter</td><td>/ˈweltər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>混乱，动乱</td><td>a welter of details<br>杂乱的一堆细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report buried its main point in a welter of small details.  这份报告把要点埋在了杂乱的琐碎细节之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 original work</td><td>/əˈrɪdʒənəl ˈwɜrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原创作品</td><td>a genuine original work<br>真正的原创作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics debated whether the play was an original work.  批评家争论这部剧是否为原创作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>partition</b></td><td>/pɑːrˈtɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>分割，划分</td><td>the partition of the estate<br>地产的分割</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers argued for years over the partition of the family land.  兄弟们为家族土地的分割争执了多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bifurcation</td><td>/ˌbaɪfərˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分岔，一分为二</td><td>the bifurcation of the river<br>河流的分岔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sharp bifurcation of the path confused the hikers.  小径突然分岔，把徒步者弄糊涂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breakup</td><td>/ˈbreɪˌkʌp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>解体，分裂</td><td>the breakup of the empire<br>帝国的解体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still argue about the breakup of the old empire.  史学家仍在争论那个旧帝国的解体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleavage</td><td>/ˈkliːvədʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，裂开</td><td>a deep political cleavage<br>深刻的政治分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A deep cleavage split the party into two hostile wings.  一道深刻的裂痕把该党分成两个敌对派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disunion</td><td>/dɪsˈjuːnjən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，不和</td><td>the threat of disunion<br>分裂的威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debate over slavery pushed the nation toward disunion.  关于奴隶制的争论把这个国家推向分裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 division</td><td>/dɪˈvɪʒən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不和；分开，部门</td><td>deep division over policy<br>政策上的深刻分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is deep division within the committee about the budget.  委员会内部在预算上存在深刻分歧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 schism</td><td>/ˈskɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，派系对立</td><td>a bitter schism<br>严重的分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bitter schism split the party into two hostile factions.  一场激烈的分裂把该党撕成两个敌对派系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scission</del></td><td>/ˈsɪʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>切断，分离，分裂</td><td>a clean scission<br>干净的切断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty produced a clean scission between the two neighboring provinces.  那份条约使两个相邻省份彻底分离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sundering</td><td>/ˈsʌndərɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分离，割裂</td><td>the sundering of old ties<br>旧关系的割裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war brought a painful sundering of old family ties.  战争带来了旧日家族关系的痛苦割裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unification</td><td>/ˌjuːnəfəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>统一，联合</td><td>the unification of the country<br>国家的统一</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty prepared the way for the unification of the states.  这项条约为各邦统一铺平了道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 union</td><td>/ˈjuːnjən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>联合，结合；工会</td><td>a lasting union of states<br>各州的持久联合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two colonies formed a lasting union after long talks.  两块殖民地经长期谈判形成持久联合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>patent</b></td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apparent</td><td>/əˈperənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的；表面上的</td><td>for no apparent reason<br>没有明显原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It soon became apparent that the machine had never worked.  很快就明显看出这台机器从未运转过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blatant</td><td>/ˈbleɪtnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公然的</td><td>a blatant lie<br>赤裸裸的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report exposed a blatant disregard for safety rules.  报告揭露了对安全规则的公然无视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conspicuous</td><td>/kənˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显而易见的；吸引人的</td><td>conspicuous consumption<br>炫耀性消费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bright red jacket made him conspicuous in the gray winter crowd.  他鲜红的夹克让他在灰暗的冬日人群中格外显眼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evident</td><td>/ˈevɪdənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显然的</td><td>clearly evident<br>显而易见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for music was evident from a very early age.  她的音乐才华从很小就显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flagrant</td><td>/ˈfleɪɡrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然的，明显恶劣的</td><td>a flagrant violation<br>公然的违规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company committed a flagrant violation of basic safety rules.  这家公司公然违反了基本安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obvious</td><td>/ˈɑːbviəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显而易见的</td><td>an obvious mistake<br>明显的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The answer seemed obvious once she explained the method.  她解释了方法后，答案似乎显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concealed</td><td>/kənˈsiːld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，暗藏的</td><td>a concealed entrance<br>一个隐蔽的入口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A concealed door behind the shelves led to the cellar.  书架后一扇隐蔽的门通向地窖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hidden</td><td>/ˈhɪdən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，隐蔽的</td><td>hidden costs<br>隐藏的费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract contained hidden fees no one had mentioned.  合同里含有没人提过的隐藏费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invisible</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪzəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>看不见的，无形的</td><td>invisible to the naked eye<br>肉眼看不见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Radio waves are invisible yet carry sound across continents.  无线电波看不见，却把声音传过大洲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pathological</b></td><td>/ˌpæθəˈlɑːdʒɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>病态的；病理的</td><td>a pathological liar<br>病态的说谎者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pathological fear of failure kept him from trying anything new.  他对失败的病态恐惧使他不敢尝试任何新事物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abnormal</td><td>/æbˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，不正常的</td><td>abnormal test results<br>异常的检测结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan showed nothing abnormal in her lungs.  扫描显示她的肺部没有异常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aberrant</td><td>/æˈberənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，非常规的</td><td>aberrant behavior<br>异常行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors were puzzled by the patient's aberrant test results.  医生对病人异常的化验结果感到困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anomalous</td><td>/əˈnɑːmələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平常的，异常的</td><td>anomalous readings<br>异常的读数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team discarded a few anomalous readings from the data.  团队剔除了数据中几个异常读数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morbid</td><td>/ˈmɔːrbɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>病态的，阴郁不健康的</td><td>a morbid fascination<br>病态的迷恋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She developed a morbid fascination with old newspaper reports of unsolved crimes.  她对旧报纸上未解案件的报道产生了病态的迷恋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 normal</td><td>/ˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>正常的；常态</td><td>back to normal<br>恢复正常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everything returned to normal after the power was restored.  供电恢复后一切都回归正常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>patina</b></td><td>/pəˈtiːnə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>外表；（由内而外散发的）氛围，气场</td><td>a patina of respectability<br>一层体面的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of careful manners gave the family a patina of respectability.  多年谨慎的举止给这家人涂上了一层体面的外表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 façade</td><td>/fəˈsɑd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>假象，表面；建筑正面</td><td>a façade of calm<br>平静的假象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind a façade of calm, the company was nearly bankrupt.  在平静的表象背后，这家公司已近破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hull</td><td>/ˈhʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>外壳；去壳</td><td>hull the strawberries<br>给草莓去萼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hulled the strawberries while the water came to a boil.  她在水烧开的时候给草莓去萼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skin</td><td>/ˈskɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>剥皮，去皮</td><td>skin a rabbit<br>给兔子剥皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He learned to skin and clean a fish as a boy.  他小时候就学会了给鱼剥皮清理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veneer</td><td>/vəˈnɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>虚饰，假象；薄薄的表层</td><td>a veneer of politeness<br>一层礼貌的虚饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His charm was only a veneer over real cruelty.  他的魅力只是掩盖真正残忍的一层虚饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 air</td><td>/ˈer/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>神态，气派；空气</td><td>an air of authority<br>威严的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old doctor had an unmistakable air of authority.  这位老医生有一种不容错认的威严气派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambience</td><td>/ˈæmbiəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>氛围，环境气氛</td><td>a relaxed ambience<br>轻松的氛围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft lamps gave the café a relaxed evening ambience.  柔和的灯光给咖啡馆带来轻松的夜间氛围。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aroma</td><td>/əˈroʊmə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（尤指食物的）香气</td><td>the aroma of fresh coffee<br>新鲜咖啡的香气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aroma of baking bread drew customers into the little shop.  烤面包的香气把顾客引进了这家小店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 atmosphere</td><td>/ˈætməsˌfɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>气氛，情调</td><td>a tense atmosphere<br>紧张的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tense atmosphere filled the room before the verdict.  宣判前，屋里弥漫着紧张的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 climate</td><td>/ˈklaɪmət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>风气，氛围；气候</td><td>the political climate<br>政治气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The current political climate discourages honest debate.  当前的政治气候不利于诚实的辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flavor</td><td>/ˈfleɪvər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>特色，韵味；味道</td><td>the flavor of the place<br>这个地方的韵味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market gave the whole street its distinctive flavor.  这个市场赋予整条街独特的韵味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 halo</td><td>/ˈheɪloʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>光环，荣光；光圈</td><td>a halo of respectability<br>体面的光环</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A halo of heroism surrounded the young captain for years.  多年间，一层英雄的光环笼罩着这位年轻船长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 odor</td><td>/ˈoʊdər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>气息，味道；名声</td><td>an odor of scandal<br>丑闻的气息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An odor of scandal clung to the firm for a decade.  丑闻的气息在这家公司周围萦绕了十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smell</td><td>/ˈsmel/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>气味，气息</td><td>the smell of old money<br>旧富人家的气息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library had the comfortable smell of old paper.  这座图书馆有旧纸张那种令人安心的气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temper</td><td>/ˈtempər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>缓和，调节；脾气</td><td>temper justice with mercy<br>以仁慈缓和公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge tempered strict justice with unexpected mercy.  法官以意外的仁慈缓和了严苛的公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vibration</td><td>/vaɪˈbreɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>气场，感觉；振动</td><td>pick up good vibrations<br>感受到好气场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She picked up friendly vibrations from the whole team.  她从整个团队感受到友好的气场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 essential quality</td><td>/ɪˈsɛnʃəl ˈkwɑlɪti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>本质特性，根本属性</td><td>the essential quality of jazz<br>爵士乐的本质特性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Improvisation is the essential quality of early jazz.  即兴演奏是早期爵士乐的本质特性。</td></tr>
